reactions · 56 shares | Four irresistible rib recipes packed with bold flavor: sticky Asian garlic, sweet chili, Korean BBQ, and classic barbecue. Tender, juicy, and finger-licking good! 🍖🔥  Sticky Asian Garlic Ribs Ingredients  * 2 racks pork ribs * 1/4 cup soy sauce * 1/4 cup hoisin sauce * 3 tbsp honey * 5 garlic cloves, minced * 1 tbsp grated ginger * 1 tbsp rice vinegar * 1 tsp sesame oil  Directions  1. Mix soy sauce, hoisin, honey, garlic, ginger, vinegar, and sesame oil. 2. Marinate ribs for 1 hour. 3. Bake at 300°F for 2–2.5 hours. 4. Brush with extra sauce and broil until sticky. 5.
